# VIDEO 003 — R4 REMAINING 101 PROMPTS

Status: **CANONICAL / NO GENERATION AUTHORIZED**
Date: 2026-10-04

Math:
- 120 total
- 19 Test30 PASS = frozen
- 11 Test30 REJECT = rewritten
- 90 never generated = rewritten
- **101 R4 prompts total**

R4 is derived from real Test30 failures. It supersedes R3 only for non-PASS frames.

## Global R4 rules
One focal point. Maximum two factual refs. Readable evidence. No logo board. No tiny pasted source. No equal tiles. No generic paper-on-dark-background default. Hard bottom-20% subtitle safe. Adjacent composition variation. Allegations stay allegations. No paid generation authorized by this file.

## F003 / B003 / G003

**ORIGIN:** REJECT_REPAIR  
**HEADLINE:** `WHY DISTROKID?`  
**VISUAL FAMILY:** IDENTIFIER_RELATIONSHIP  
**LAYOUT:** CENTER_NODE_PIPELINE / RIGHT_HEAVY  
**LEGAL MODE:** STANDARD

### NARRATION PURPOSE
Universal did not just go after whoever uploaded the music It sued the company in the middle

### DIRECTOR INTENT
DistroKid is the single center node. Complaint is the evidence anchor at left. No third equal source. The question must read before any detail.

- VS001

- Use this exact prepared image as factual evidence; do not synthesize a substitute.

### COMPOSITION
One center node only. Evidence enters from left, DistroKid occupies the center, one restrained outbound direction exits right. No equal tiles.

### HARD ANTI-WASTE RULES
- One beat = one visual idea = one dominant focal point.
- Maximum two factual references.
- Primary source must be visually meaningful, never a tiny pasted screenshot.
- No equal tiles, logo boards, contact sheets, evidence piles or dashboards.
- Important article/document/UI source must be tightly cropped to meaningful readable content.
- Logos are identifiers only, never wallpaper.
- Bottom 20% genuinely empty for captions: no headline, face, key evidence, tiny text, number/date or important edge.
- Do not default to “source/paper on a dark background.”
- Generated text = exact locked headline only, except unavoidable source-native text.
- No fake legal text, fake UI, source lookalikes, orange collage blocks, black caption boxes, neon/glossy ads, random desk clutter, gavels or Lady Justice.

### LEGAL SAFETY
Stay strictly within locked narration and attached evidence. Do not add unsupported conclusions.
